<a id="global-guards"></a>

**Load-bearing fact:** there is **no global `JwtAuthGuard`** (global guards above). A route is authenticated only by an authenticating guard on its own method or class; one with neither that nor `@Public()` is reachable without a credential and is counted ⚠️ UNGUARDED.

<a id="legend"></a>

## Legend

| Symbol | Meaning |
|---|---|
| ✅ | `JwtAuthGuard` on the method or its class, and no `@Public()` |
| 🔑 | a non-JWT credential guard decides — service key (`ServiceKeyGuard`), MCP house key (`McpCredentialAuthGuard`), or the relay door (`RelayDoorGuard`, JWT or service key). These ignore `@Public()`, so the route is **not** open |
| 🌐 | **public** — explicit `@Public()` and no guard that ignores it. Deliberate, not a defect; the trace says why |
| ⚠️ | **UNGUARDED** — no authenticating guard and no `@Public()`. Reachable without a credential |
| `(c)` / `(m)` | declared on the class / on the method |
| `untraced` | no trace entry yet: the column is unknown, **not** empty |

Columns: **Auth** · **Method** · **Path** (under the global prefix) · **Guards · flags** (guards without the `Guard` suffix; `@AllowsNoHouse` etc.; rate limits) · **Role** (trace `role_check`, else the `@Roles` declaration) · **House scope** (trace verdict + evidence) · **Service** · **Data** · **Inputs** (`@Param` / `@Query` / `@Body` DTO) · **Handler** (`file:line` of the verb decorator, relative to `apps/api-gateway/src/`) · **Notes**. Sections are per feature directory, then per controller class; rows sorted by path, then method.

### `InventoryLedgerController` (8) — `inventory-ledger/inventory-ledger.controller.ts:39` `@Controller("inventory-ledger")`

| Auth | Method | Path | Guards · flags | Role | House scope | Service | Data | Inputs | Handler | Notes |

| <a id="get-inventory-ledger-inventory-inventoryid-balance"></a>✅ | `GET` | `/inventory-ledger/inventory/:inventoryId/balance` | JwtAuth(c) | none | **unscoped** `inventory-ledger/inventory-ledger.service.ts:475-481` `supabase/migrations/20260805000000_baseline_from_production.sql:661-664` — FINDING: restaurantId is received but never used — the RPC is called with p_inventory_id only, no ownership check precedes it, and get_inventory_balance_at filters inventory_transactions by inventory_id alone (baseline:661-664; no later migration redefines it). Exploit: a member of house A calls GET /api/v1/inventory-ledger/inventory/<house B inventory uuid>/balance?asOf=2100-01-01T00:00:00Z (optionally &stockType=shadow) and receives house B's latest quantity_after for that item, i.e. B's current live (or shadow) stock. Read-only; needs B's item uuid. Fix: call assertInventoryBelongsToRestaurant first, as the sibling routes do. Checked against all 281 migration files: get_inventory_balance_at is defined only at 20260805000000_baseline_from_production.sql:655-670 (plpgsql STABLE, not SECURITY DEFINER, no later replace/grant/revoke); no guard compares :inventoryId (assertTenantMatch reads only restaurantId/restaurant_id). Read-only, one integer, needs the foreign item uuid. | `inventory-ledger/inventory-ledger.service.ts:469` InventoryLedgerService.getBalanceAt | read-only · tables inventory_transactions · rpc get_inventory_balance_at | param inventoryId; query `GetBalanceAtQueryDto` | `inventory-ledger/inventory-ledger.controller.ts:179` getBalanceAt | An unknown id answers balance 0, indistinguishable from a real zero. |

| <a id="post-pos-hub-webhook-provider-restaurantid"></a>🌐 | `POST` | `/pos-hub/webhook/:provider/:restaurantId` | JwtAuth(c) @Public(m) | custom `pos-hub/pos-hub.controller.ts:102` | public `pos-hub/pos-hub.controller.ts:102-107` `pos-hub/pos-hub.service.ts:393` `pos-hub/pos-hub.service.ts:414-426` — @Public; HMAC-SHA256 over '<provider>:<restaurantId>.'+rawBody on a scoped secret, fails closed with no secret. The path house then drives every read/write: mappings (pos-hub.service.ts:591), house-filtered inventory gate (:626, :777), pos_checks upsert keyed on restaurant_id (:507, :529), apply_stock_movement p_restaurant_id (:876). | `pos-hub/pos-hub.service.ts:438` PosHubService.ingest | writes · tables pos_checks, pos_item_mappings, restaurant_tables, restaurant_inventory, pos_unresolved_lines, wine_consumption_log · rpc apply_stock_movement, record_glass_pour | param provider, restaurantId; body `unknown` | `pos-hub/pos-hub.controller.ts:79` webhook | @Public() makes JwtAuthGuard stand aside; Secret ladder per_connection -> per_provider -> legacy POS_HUB_WEBHOOK_SECRET (pos-hub.service.ts:314-318). On the legacy rung the signature covers rawBody only (:414-417), so a body signed for house A also verifies on house B's path: a captured webhook can be replayed into another house and deplete its stock through B's own mappings. On the per-provider rung one secret signs for every house of that provider. Only the per-connection rung binds the door to one house. Which rungs are set in production is not visible from code. Closed checks deplete stock; voids reverse with a derived idempotency key. Stock idempotency key `pos:<source>:<externalCheckId>:<item>:<line>` (pos-hub.service.ts:813) has no house, and apply_stock_movement / record_glass_pour dedupe on it globally (supabase/migrations/20260912163000_a_stock_write_names_its_house.sql:203-205; baseline_from_production.sql:1142-1143; unique index uq_inventory_transactions_idem :11852, unique constraint pour_events_idempotency_key_key :7328): a second house's identical source/check/item/line silently moves no stock (an idempotent hit returns like a real movement); realistic for generic_webhook sources, unlikely for provider GUID check ids. |

Settled deliveries are skipped and their timer cancelled (delivery-clock.service.ts:540-553). |

EXPLOIT 1: {createDelivery:true, orderId:<house B order>, lines:[...]} creates a house-A delivery on B's order; notifyIfItDiffers -> scanAgainstTheOrder reads B's procurement_order_items .eq('order_id') with no house filter (delivery.service.ts:1692-1708) and the response's differsOnLines (plus a house-A notification carrying orderId) reports how many of B's order lines differ from the count. EXPLOIT 2: providerId:<house B provider> plants a foreign provider that GET :id/canonical then names with its tax id. The foreign house's shelf is not at risk (booking passes p_restaurant_id, delivery-stock.service.ts:535, and apply_stock_movement refuses another house's item, 20260912163000_a_stock_write_names_its_house.sql:160-162), but the foreign order id is stamped on this house's inventory_transactions.order_id (delivery-stock.service.ts:523; 20260912163000:254-260) and deliveryHasBookedOrder (delivery-stock.service.ts:130-142) reads that ledger by order_id alone, so the other house's markDelivered (procurement.service.ts:5214), recordDoorReceipt (receiving.service.ts:441) and nameDeliveredItem (delivery-item-to-name.ts:386) skip booking its stock; deliveryId is checked via byId (.eq restaurant_id). Despite the Swagger text ('Nothing here writes stock') the handler books stock at the door when a delivery is attached (documents.controller.ts:941-946). Any member may call. Optional photo stored in vendor-attachments. |

Exploit: any authenticated member, any role, POSTs /api/v1/recurring-orders/<own house>/execute-check -> every house's due schedules execute now, and calls concurrent with each other or with the cron can process a due row twice (the advance at :1051-1058 comes after createOrder). | `procurement/recurring-orders.service.ts:641` RecurringOrdersService.executeDueRecurringOrders | writes · tables recurring_orders, procurement_orders, procurement_order_items, restaurant_inventory, providers, restaurants, events, calendar_events · ext orchestrator, rabbitmq | — | `procurement/recurring-orders.controller.ts:154` manualExecuteCheck | Summary says '(dev/test)' but it is not env-gated or role-gated.
